### greet-zh

```

```


### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「信頼性を重視する通信」、UDPは「速さを重視する通信」です。

TCPはデータが確実に届くよう再送や順序の整列を行うので遅れやすく、UDPは再送などをせず送りっぱなしにするので速く届きますが、届かないことや順番が入れ替わることがあります。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para limpiar tus commits locales antes de compartirlos, y merge para integrar una rama que ya está publicada o que otras personas usan. Nunca hagas rebase de ramas públicas, porque reescribe el historial que otros pueden tener.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、有序的字节流，先握手再传、丢了会重发；UDP 不保证送达和顺序，但没有连接和重传的开销，速度快、延迟低。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when the branch is yours and unshared, to keep history linear before you share or merge it. Merge when others have pulled the branch or you want the true history of when work was integrated.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接在 `bun test` 后面加上文件路径就行:

```
bun test src/utils/parse.test.ts
```

也可以只写文件名或一部分路径,Bun 会匹配:

```
bun test parse.test
```

如果想只跑某个 `test(...)` 用例,用 `-t` 按名字过滤:

```
bun test parse.test.ts -t "空字符串"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to a database before that change is applied to the main data files. If the system crashes midway, the database replays the log on restart to recover committed changes and discard incomplete ones.
```

### board-offer-go-en

```
(earlier) user: Run hawdb's write benchmark every night and tell me how it went.
(earlier) partner ack: On it: every night at 23:00.  → task "Nightly hawdb write benchmark" working; its report: Night 3: 182k writes/s, p99 4.1 ms, level with last night (181k, 4.0 ms). Compaction stalls: 0.
(earlier) partner: Night 3: 182k writes/s at p99 4.1 ms, level with last night; no compaction stalls.
(earlier) Ducter offered on its own card: keep task "bench"'s rounds on a board?
--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] Done: the board is on your shelf as "Nightly hawdb writes". It has the two nights so far, Night 2 and Night 3, and each new run will add to it.
  [an app card under the message: made "Nightly hawdb writes", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1552 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="wps">—</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">writes/s</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
  <div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>Nothing yet. Each night's result lands here.</div>
</main>
<script>
function fmt(n){ return Number(n).toLocaleString('en-US'); }
function render(nights){
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
  if (!Array.isArray(nights) || !nights.length) {
    list.innerHTML = ''; empty.hidden = false;
    document.getElementById('wps').innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">No runs yet</span>';
    document.getElementById('sub').textContent = 'writes/s, p99 latency';
    return;
  }
  empty.hidden = true;
  var last = nights[nights.length - 1];
  document.getElementById('wps').textContent = fmt(last.writes);
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent = 'writes/s · p99 ' + last.p99 + ' ms · stalls ' + last.stalls;
  list.innerHTML = nights.slice().reverse().map(function(n){
    return '<div class="k-row"><div class="k-row-main">' + n.night +
      '</div><div class="k-row-sub">p99 ' + n.p99 + ' ms · stalls ' + n.stalls +
      '</div><div class="k-row-value">' + fmt(n.writes) + '/s</div></div>';
  }).join('');
}
Promise.resolve(ducter.get('nights')).then(render);
ducter.onChange(function(){ Promise.resolve(ducter.get('nights')).then(render); });
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"nights":[{"night":"Night 2","writes":181000,"p99":4,"stalls":0},{"night":"Night 3","writes":182000,"p99":4.1,"stalls":0}]}
  [Ducter passed the user's answer to task bench] [Ducter] The person now keeps this work's rounds in their small app "Nightly hawdb writes" (app a48b0b80b). Each round, write what stands now into it with app_data (app a48b0b80b, set {key: value}; null removes a key); the app reads the keys nights. Write whole values (a key's value is replaced), once a round. Then don't report_to_main for a round that only updated it: report only when something n
  [also used tools] app
```

```
}

function persist() { return ducter.set('expenses', items); }

    day: $('d').value.trim() || today(),
    what,
    amt: Math.round(amt * 100) / 100
  });

  $('a').value = '';
  $('msg').textContent = '';
  $('add').open = false;
  persist();
  render();
});

$('list').addEventListener('click', e => {
  const del = e.target.closest('[data-del]');
  if (del) {
    items = items.filter(i => i.id !== del.dataset.del);
    sel = null;
    persist();
    render();
    return;
  }
  const row = e.target.closest('.k-row');
  if (row) {
    sel = sel === row.dataset.id ? null : row.dataset.id;
    render();
  }
});

ducter.onChange(() => load());
load();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}
```

```
.k-sum-sub{font-size:13px;color:var(--d-muted)}
</style>
<script>
function fmt(n){return "¥"+Number(n).toLocaleString("zh-CN")}
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  const sum=xs.reduce((a,x)=>a+Number(x.yuan||0),0);
  document.getElementById("total").textContent=xs.length?fmt(sum):"¥0";
  document.getElementById("count").textContent=xs.length?"共 "+xs.length+" 笔":"还没记";
  const list=document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML="";
  for(const x of xs){
    const row=document.createElement("div");
    row.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("div");
    main.className="k-row-main";
    main.textContent=x.what;
    const sub=document.createElement("div");
    sub.className="k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent=x.day;
    const val=document.createElement("div");
    val.className="k-row-value";
    val.textContent=fmt(x.yuan);
    row.append(main,sub,val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
}
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();
```

```
var feeds = [], sel = null;
function el(tag, cls, text){var e=document.createElement(tag); if(cls) e.className=cls; if(text!=null) e.textContent=text; return e;}
function load(){ feeds = ducter.get('feeds') || []; render(); }
function render(){
  var todays = feeds.filter(function(f){return f.d===today;}).sort(function(a,b){return a.t<b.t?-1:1;});
  var total = todays.reduce(function(s,f){return s+Number(f.ml);},0);
  var total … gth + ' 次 · 最近 ' + todays[todays.length-1].t : '记下第一次喝奶';
  list.innerHTML = '';
  if(!todays.length){ list.appendChild(el('div','k-empty','今天还没有记录')); }
  todays.forEach(function(f){
    var row = el('div','k-row');
    row.appendChild(el('div','k-row-main', f.t));
    row.appendChild(el('div','k-row-value', f.ml + ' ml'));
    row.addEventListener('click', function(){ sel = (sel===f) ? null : f; render(); });

  });
  ducter.summary(todays.length ? total + ' ml · ' + todays.length + ' 次' : '今天还没记');
}
document.getElementById('add').addEventListener('click', function(){
  var t = document.getElementById('t').value.trim();
  var ml = parseFloat(document.getElementById('ml').value);
  if(!t || !(ml>0)) return;
  feeds.push({d: today, t: t, ml: ml});
  ducter.set('feeds', feeds);
  document.getElementById('t').value=''; document.getElementById('ml').value='';
  render();
});
ducter.onChange(load);
load();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"d":"2026-11-23","t":"06:10","ml":120},{"d":"2026-11-23","t":"09:05","ml":90}]}
```

```
<h2 class="k-h">Open PRs</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
</div>

<style>
  .k-row { display: flex; align-items: flex-start; gap: 10px; cursor: pointer; position: relative; }
  .k-row input { width: 18px; height: 18px; margin-top: 2px; flex: none; accent-color: var(--d-accent, #2f6fed); }
  .k-row-main { display: block; }
  .k-row-sub { display: block; color: var(--d-muted, #888); }
  .k-row-done .k-row-main { text-decoration: line-through; color: var(--d-muted, #888); }
</style>

<script>
  var listEl = document.getElementById('list');
  var state = {};
  var items = [];

  function render() {
    var waiting = items.filter(function (p) { return !state[p.id]; }).length;
    document.getElementById('count').textContent = waiting;
    document.getElementById('sub').textContent = waiting === 0
      ? 'All of them have landed'
      : waiting + ' still waiting';
    if (window.ducter && ducter.summary) {
      ducter.summary(waitin … t('label');
      row.className = 'k-row' + (state[p.id] ? ' k-row-done' : '');
      var box = document.createElement('input');
      box.type = 'checkbox';
      box.checked = !!state[p.id];
      box.addEventListener('change', function () {
        state[p.id] = box.checked;
        if (window.ducter && ducter.set) ducter.set('ticked', state);
        draw();
        render();
      });
      var text = document.createElement('span');
      var main = document.createElement('span');
      main.className = 'k-row-main';
      main.textContent = p.title;
      var sub = document.createElement('span');
      sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
      sub.textContent = p.sub;
      text.appendChild(main);
      text.appendChild(sub);
      row.appendChild(box);
      row.appendChild(text);
      listEl.appendChild(row);
    });
    render();
  }

  function load() {
    if (!(window.ducter && ducter.get)) { draw(); return; }
    Promise.all([ducter.get('prs'), ducter.get('ticked')]).then(function (r) {
      items = (r[0] && r[0].prs) || r[0] || [];
      state = r[1] || {};
      draw();
    });
  }

  load();
  if (window.ducter && ducter.onChange) ducter.onChange(load);
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"ducter-412","title":"wey-gu/ducter #412 · compaction cut","sub":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday","from":"Your open PRs"},{"id":"mem-10287","title":"nowledge-co/mem #10287 · retry on 502","sub":"CI failing","from":"Your open PRs"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","title":"nowledge-co/hawdb #1592 · flaky compaction test","sub":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday","from":"Your open PRs"}],"ticked":{}}
```
